7848|Welcome to Riftbound Basics featuring
7850|runes. In the Riftbound TCG, your energy
7853|to play cards comes automatically. Each
7855|player brings their own rune deck
7857|consisting of 12 cards. This consists of
7860|runes in the domains of your legend. A
7862|Draven deck, for example, can play chaos
7865|and fury runes. At the start of your
7867|turn, immediately after you have readied
7869|all your cards and received points for
7871|holding a battlefield, you may play the
7873|top two cards from your rune deck. You
7876|need runes to pay the energy and power
7878|costs on your cards. For example, for
7881|Victor Leader, you must pay four energy,
7883|which means exhausting four runes of any
7886|color and then pay a power cost. You do
7889|this by placing a rune of the color
7891|shown under the energy cost at the
7892|bottom of your rune deck. This process
7895|is also called recycling. Some cards
7898|such as Seal of Insight have no energy
7900|cost but do have a power cost. However,
7903|to ensure your energy isn't lost, you
7905|may float the energy. That is, exhaust
7907|your runes, place them under your rune
7909|deck, and later use the energy gained to
7912|play another card. Consequently, power
7914|costs have no effect on the current turn
7916|in which you play the cards, but can
7918|have a significant impact on subsequent
7920|turns as you are constantly playing with

12784|Welcome to Riftbond Basics featuring the
12787|ground rules. Riftbond is a trading card
12790|game based on the League of Legends
12791|universe in which the aim is to score
12793|eight victory points across two
12795|different battlefields. Points are
12797|scored by moving your own units onto one
12799|of the battlefields and conquering it or
12801|by holding it for more than one turn.
12803|The different car types in Riftbound are
12805|units, gears, and spells. To play these,
12808|you must pay an energy cost, which is
12810|shown in the top left corner of the
12811|card, as well as a power cost.
12814|Energy is obtained by exhausting runes.
12817|These come from the 12 card rune deck
12819|from which the top two cards are played
12821|for free at the beginning of each turn.
12823|If you exhaust your runes to pay energy,
12825|you must also pay a power cost for some
12827|cards. To do this, you simply place one
12830|of your runes bearing the symbol shown
12832|on the card under your rune deck. You
12834|can also use runes that are already
12835|exhausted for this.
12838|The domains of the runes and the cards
12839|you play in your main and sight deck are
12841|determined by your legend. Units that
12843|you play enter the board exhausted. This
12846|means they cannot move until they are
12847|readyed at the start of the next turn.
12849|Just like all other exhausted cards on
12851|your side, you initially play your units
12853|and gears into your base. There they are
12855|protected from combat. Gears can't get
12857|into combat. If you already control a
12860|battlefield, you also have the option to
12861|play your units directly from your hand
12863|onto it. This is where your units might
12866|comes into play. This is the number
12868|shown on the top right hand corner of
12870|the units. Might represents both the
12871|attack power and the defensive stat of
12874|your units. If a player moves units into
12877|an already occupied battlefield, combat
12879|stunts. Here, spells with action and
12882|reaction speed can be activated to deal
12884|damage or modify might. When combat
12886|occurs and no player wishes to activate
12888|any more spells or abilities, the total
12890|might of all units is added together and
12892|distributed amongst the opposing units.
12894|Players may decide for themselves which
12896|opposing unit they wish to damage. Once
12899|combat is over, any damage is healed by
12901|units that were involved and did not
12903|die. The game ends when a player reaches
12905|eight victory points, though this is not
12907|quite so easy to achieve. Whilst every
12910|other point simply requires conquering a

18073|the Hammer and Master Yi Wu Jun Master.
18076|Notice that all of these have the body
18077|domain in common. Master Yi uses XP
18080|passively. With higher level, he finds
18082|stronger passive effects. At level six,
18085|your units are permanently boosted, and
18087|at level 11, your units always enter
18089|ready. Of course, in the main deck, most
18092|masteries will rely on the champion
18094|unit, Master Yi, unstoppable as a win
18096|condition. This one gets cheaper the
18098|more XP you have, and at level 16, it
18101|even becomes unargetable. Combined with
18103|Master Yi, Wu Master's ability to enter
18105|ready, he can really swing games.
18108|Kha'Zix and Poppy have the unique
18110|ability to gain XP through their own
18112|legend abilities and otherwise use it
18114|like a resource. While Poppy generates
18116|card advantage by spending XP, Kha'Zix
18118|gains utility by either buffing his
18121|units or moving exhausted units away
18123|from their battlefields back to base.
18125|Since all of these three legends have
18126|the body domain in common, there's a
18128|huge variety of body cards that can get

18132|keyword. For example, hunt cards grant
18135|you an XP whenever you conquer or hold.

18140|utmost importance. Cards like Gemand
18143|Hunter, Boracious Gromp, and Master Heat
18146|Tempered can be found in all three
18148|decks, but XP can also be splashed into
18151|other decks. Grim Resolve, for example,

18155|unit three might on action speed. If the
